Only paragraphs (<hp:p>...</hp:p>) whose concatenated <hp:t> text contains a {{...}} placeholder are modified.\n- For modified paragraphs, stale layout cache (<hp:linesegarray>...</hp:linesegarray>) zipfile\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Dict, Iterable, List, Tuple\n\n\nPLACEHOLDER_RE = re.compile(r\"{{\\s*([^{}]+?)\\s*}}\")\n\n\ndef xml_escape(text: str) -> str:\n    # Minimal XML escaping for element text.\n    return (\n        text.replace(\"&\", \"&amp;\")\n        .replace(\"<\", \"&lt;\")\n        .replace(\">\", \"&gt;\")\n        .replace('\"', \"&quot;\")\n        .replace(\"'\", \"&apos;\")\n    )\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ReplacementEvent:\n    filename: str\n    paragraph_id: str | None\n    placeholder_key: str\n    value: str\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass RenderResult:\n    replacements: List[ReplacementEvent]\n    modified_paragraph_ids_by_file: Dict[str, List[str]]\n\n\ndef find_placeholders_with_context(text: str, context: int = 30) -> List[Tuple[str, int, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Return list of (raw_match, index, context_snippet).\"\"\"\n    out: List[Tuple[str, int, str]] = []\n    for m in PLACEHOLDER_RE.finditer(text):\n        start, end = m.span(0)\n        snippet = text[max(0, start - context) : min(len(text), end + context)]\n        out.append((m.group(0), start, snippet))\n    return out\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders(plain_text: str, data: Dict[str, str], *, filename: str, paragraph_id: str | None, events: List[ReplacementEvent]) -> str:\n    def _sub(m: re.Match) -> str:\n        key = m.group(1).strip()\n        if key not in data:\n            raise KeyError(f\"Missing key in JSON for placeholder: {key!r} (file={filename}, paragraph_id={paragraph_id})\")\n        value = str(data[key])\n        events.append(ReplacementEvent(filename=filename, paragraph_id=paragraph_id, placeholder_key=key, value=value))\n        return value\n\n    return PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(_sub, plain_text)\n\n\nP_RE = re.compile(r\"(<hp:p\\\\b[^>]*>)(.*?)(</hp:p>)\", re.DOTALL)\nT_RE = re.compile(r\"<hp:t>(.*?)</hp:t>\", re.DOTALL)\nPID_RE = \"\"\"Render a single XML document; returns (new_xml, modified_paragraph_ids).\"\"\"\n    modified_ids: List[str] = []\n\n    def _render_paragraph(m: re.Match) -> str:\n        open_tag, inner, close_tag = m.group(1), m.group(2), m.group(3)\n        pid_m = PID_RE.search(open_tag)\n        pid = pid_m.group(1) if pid_m else None\n\n        # Concatenate all <hp:t> text in paragraph to detect/replace placeholders,\n        # even if placeholder tokens are fragmented across runs.\n        texts = T_RE.findall(inner)\n        if not texts:\n            return m.group(0)\n\n        concatenated = \"\".join(texts)\n        if \"{{\" not in concatenated or \"}}\" not in concatenated:\n            return m.group(0)\n\n        # If the concatenated text has no placeholder matches, don't touch it.\n        if not PLACEHOLDER_RE.search(concatenated):\n            return m.group(0)\n\n        replaced_plain = replace_placeholders(\n            concatenated,\n            data,\n            filename=filename,\n            paragraph_id=pid,\n            events=events,\n        )\n\n        # Preserve the paragraph's first run style if present.\n        charpr = \"0\"\n        charpr_m = RUN_CHARPR_RE.search(inner)\n        if charpr_m:\n            charpr = charpr_m.group(1)\n\n        # Rebuild as a single run to avoid tag-fragmented placeholders and to keep the XML simple.\n        new_inner = f'<hp:run charPrIDRef=\"{xml_escape(charpr)}\"><hp:t>{xml_escape(replaced_plain)}</hp:t></hp:run>'\n\n        # Remove stale layout cache in modified paragraph (critical to avoid overlapping glyphs).\n        new_inner = LINESEGARRAY_RE.sub(\"\", new_inner)\n\n        if pid is not None:\n            modified_ids.append(pid)\n\n        return open_tag + new_inner + We also treat .hpf/.opf/etc as text.\n    low = name.lower()\n    return low.endswith((\".xml\", \".hpf\", \".opf\", \".rels\", \".txt\"))\n\n\ndef render_hwpx(template_path: str, data_path: str,
